local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

													local function v4126(p636)
														if not p636 then
															return nil
														end

														if p636 == "DisplayBlock" then
															return "DisplayBlock"
														end

														if p636 == "Button" or p636 == "Switch" or p636 == "SwitchBig" or p636 == "Lever" then
															return p636
														end

														if p636 == "Gate" then
															return "Gate"
														end

														if p636 == "Firework" then
															return nil
														end

														if t139[p636] then
															return p636
														end

														if string.find(p636, "Block") then
															return "Block"
														end

														if string.find(p636, "Wheel") and p636 ~= "Wheel" or p636 == "Motor" or p636 == "HugeMotor" then
															return "Wheel"
														end

														if string.find(p636, "JetTurbine") then
															return "JetTurbine"
														end

														if string.find(p636, "Firework") then
															return "Firework"
														end

														if p636 == "Camera" or p636 == "CameraDome" then
															return "Camera"
														end

														if p636 == "Cannon" or p636 == "MiniGun" or string.find(p636, "Harpoon") then
															return "Launcher"
														end

														if t139[p636] then
															return p636
														end

														return "Block"
													end

													isCFrameInZone = function(p637, p638)

														local Position2 = p638.Position

								blockConversion.TriangleBox = function(p847, p848, p849, p850, p851)

								blockConversion.Convert = function(p870, p871)

									local n462 = 0
